! Title: RespChla - Anime GIGANTE V1
! Description: Bloqueo masivo de anime, manga, manhwa/manhua, streaming, torrents, mirrors, bases de datos y plataformas oficiales.
! Generated: 2026-07-31
! Syntax: AdGuard DNS blocklist (||domain^) + conservative regex rules
! Exact domains: 12,381
! Regex rules: 3
! Active blocking rules: 12,384
! Homepage: https://github.com/RespChla/SchoolList
!
! Sources:
! - UT1 manga category (CC BY-SA)
! - FMHY Anime Streaming, Anime Downloading, Anime Torrenting, current aggregators and multilingual entries
! - NextDNS streaming-video + piracy candidates that commonly carry anime
! - Awesome Anime Sources catalogue
! - Aniyomi/Anikku extension ecosystems used as discovery references
! - Manual review of official platforms, Spanish/Latin sites, trackers, manga readers and rotating mirrors
!
! Safety:
! - Shared infrastructure roots (Google, YouTube, GitHub, Cloudflare, AWS, Vercel, Netlify, etc.) are not blocked wholesale.
! - Specific tenants on shared hosts may be blocked when identified as dedicated mirrors.
! - Core educational platforms are excluded rather than allowed with @@, so this file cannot override another intentional policy.
! - General platforms known to carry anime (Netflix, Disney+, Prime Video, Hulu, Tubi, Pluto, Max) are included.
! - YouTube is not blocked because DNS cannot distinguish anime channels from educational video content.
!
